Use when an independent evaluator can materially improve correctness, scope discipline, regression safety, evidence honesty, design quality, or general-purpose skill/prompt behavior. ([LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch](https://github.com/LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch/tree/HEAD/thalarch-mode/skills/thalarch-teacher) · ★6)
- **thalarch-test**: Designs high-value regression, property, integration, fuzz, and risk-based mutation tests for behavior changes. Use after a root cause is known or a feature contract exists. - **the-agent-couldn-t-find-the-leaked-env-4c2745344ce020fb**: A worker-wide environment audit for leaked env vars: when a test sets an environment variable and never unsets it, every later test on the same worker sees the polluted value, and reading the failing test's code will never reveal it. - **the-agent-keeps-chasing-the-failing-te-94d464f01df16d58**: An upstream-first debugging discipline for state leaks: when the failing test's logs show nothing wrong, the corruption came from an earlier passing test, and more log-reading on the victim is wasted effort. - **the-agent-leaked-its-nvd-api-key-into-aae7850fe7f03bb0**: Recovers from an NVD API key leaked into agent logs via rotation, log redaction, and secret-store sourcing. - **the-agent-s-browser-automation-failed-d4e4b85e6babdc03**: Fixes an agent browser automation failing on a bank portal login when the MFA push never arrives: switch the step to a channel the portal supports unattended, add a human-approval checkpoint, or move to an API or file channel. - **the-agent-s-codemod-converted-200-file-dedbf43017351526**: Fixes resuming a codemod that crashed after converting 200 files, leaving the repo half on the old API. - **the-agent-s-credential-expired-mid-swe-9c006ea888818e3e**: Restores a triage agent whose credential expired mid-sweep and broke mailbox access, by rotating to a longer-lived credential model and adding expiry monitoring. - **the-agent-s-csv-parser-broke-on-the-ba-ea94531fe60d4b10**: Fixes an agent CSV parser breaking on a bank's semicolon-delimited file with quoted newlines: configure the parser for the real dialect, delimiter, quoting, and line endings, instead of assuming commas. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/the-agent-s-data-migration-copied-60-p-6f011f107147aeda))
- **the-agent-s-debian-security-tracker-sc-ba1dad8ea8f3736b**: Fixes Debian security tracker scrapers that broke when the tracker moved to a JSON format by switching from HTML scraping to the tracker's JSON data endpoint. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/the-agent-s-debian-security-tracker-sc-ba1dad8ea8f3736b))

- **the-agent-s-defender-api-session-broke-6f5a282588a0736e**: Restores a triage agent Defender API session broken by a password or credential rotation it did not know about, by moving the agent to rotation-proof credentials. Use when API access breaks immediately after an account credential change. Not for rotations the agent was notified of and updated for. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/the-agent-s-defender-api-session-broke-6f5a282588a0736e))
- **the-agent-s-diff-flagged-every-defined-265a4cf32dc3d0d9**: Fixes diffs that flag every defined term as changed when the counterparty just capitalized them all: casing treated as substantive. It shows how to compare defined terms case-insensitively and keep casing as display-only. Use it when a capitalization pass floods your redline with term changes that mean nothing. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/the-agent-s-diff-flagged-every-defined-265a4cf32dc3d0d9))
- **the-agent-s-diff-flagged-the-entire-si-27f90f6c252f9a5f**: Fixes diffs that flag the whole signature block as deleted when the counterparty switches page size, like Letter to A4, shifting every page break. It shows how to diff at the paragraph level ignoring pagination, and how to treat signature blocks as structured content. Use it when a page-size or margin change makes your redline agent report deletions that are really just repagination. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/the-agent-s-diff-flagged-the-entire-si-27f90f6c252f9a5f))
- **the-agent-s-diff-treated-a-reordered-e-21cf4258b9128baa**: Fixes exhibit-list diffs that report a reorder as 12 deletions plus 12 additions: the agent never realigned the list items. It shows how to match list items by content across versions, detect moves, and compare lists independent of order. Use it when reordered exhibits, schedules, or numbered lists produce deletion-heavy diffs that scare reviewers. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/the-agent-s-diff-treated-a-reordered-e-21cf4258b9128baa))
- **the-agent-s-exploitability-assessment-214b8933cbd14a91**: Fixes an LLM triage agent that contradicts itself on whether a vulnerable function is reachable by making every verdict evidence-bound: no call path, no 'not reachable.' ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/the-agent-s-exploitability-assessment-214b8933cbd14a91))
- **the-agent-s-exploitability-verdict-rel-1f75d8cb6557acec**: Use when exploitability verdicts change because advisory prose was reworded, not because facts changed. Covers evidence-backed verdicts, ignoring prose-only diffs, and versioning verdicts with evidence hashes. Not for new technical information in an advisory update. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/the-agent-s-exploitability-verdict-rel-1f75d8cb6557acec))
- **the-agent-s-extraction-returned-comple-c2e66af36c3943f3**: Fixes false 'complete' reports in contract extraction: the agent said extraction was complete while the last 30 pages of schedules never entered context. It shows how to gate the complete flag on an independent page count, verify pages-in equals pages-processed, and make incompleteness impossible to misreport. Use it when extraction reports success but you suspect trailing pages were never read. - **the-agent-s-flyway-run-lost-its-connec-8eb613a6a073e291**: Fixes a Flyway run where the schema history row committed but the data copy did not finish after a connection loss. Use when flyway_schema_history disagrees with the real table contents. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/the-agent-s-flyway-run-lost-its-connec-8eb613a6a073e291))
- **the-agent-s-ignore-rule-expired-silent-21099674b982de5c**: Use when expired suppressions return as a flood of new findings with no warning. - **the-agent-s-ioc-extractor-failed-on-ba-46a8562134d56872**: Fixes an agent IOC extractor failing on base64-encoded message bodies, by decoding MIME parts before extraction. - **the-agent-s-nightly-fleet-scan-only-fi-52ec92c9e6f40fdb**: Fixes nightly fleet scans that never finish in the maintenance window by parallelizing workers, caching the vulnerability DB once per host, and skipping unchanged image digests. - **the-agent-s-nvd-cve-feed-parser-broke-80f988cb47f12703**: Fixes NVD feed parsers that silently match zero CVEs after the NVD 2.0 schema change by updating the configurations node path and adding a zero-match canary alert. - **the-agent-s-one-pr-per-cve-policy-crea-23098480ec6fcdd8**: Replaces a one-PR-per-CVE policy with root-cause grouping: when many CVEs trace to one shared transitive dependency, a single bump PR closes them all.
